+ "Converts a float tensor to a quantized tensor by applying a scale/zero-point/min transform:\n\nresult = round((input - min) / scale) + zero_point\n\nThe exact operation sequence (where ftype is the input type):\n\nr_sub: ftype = sub(input, min) r_div: ftype = real_div(r_sub, scale) r_rnd: ftype = round(r_div) Z: ftype = cast(zero_point) r_zero: ftype = add(r_rnd, Z) r_clip: ftype = clip(r_zero, min_value<dtype>, max_value<dtype>) result: dtype = cast(r_clip)\n\nSupported quantized types: `ui1/bool`, `ui2`, `si2`, `ui4`, `si4`, `ui8`, `si8`\n\n**Per-axis quantization:** If `axis` is provided, `scale`, `zero_point`, and `min` must be 0-D or 1-D tensors that will be broadcast along the specified axis.\n\nNote: Unlike DequantizeOp, QuantizeOp does NOT support N-D blockwise quantization.\n\n**Per-tensor quantization:** If `axis` is not defined and `scale` is a scalar, the operation is per-tensor.\n\n**Specialized formats:** - MX scaling: `f4e2m1`, `f6e3m2`, `f6e2m3`, `f8e4m3`, `f8e5m2` with `f8e8m0` scale and blocksize 32 - NVFP4: `f4e2m1` with `f8e4m3` scale and blocksize 16"

- "Converts a float tensor to a quantized tensor by applying a scale/zero-point transform:\n\nresult = round(input / scale) + zero_point\n\nThe exact operation sequence (where ftype is the input type):\n\nr_div: ftype = real_div(input, scale) r_rnd: ftype = round(r_div) Z: ftype = cast(zero_point) r_zero: ftype = add(r_rnd, Z) r_clip: ftype = clip(r_zero, min_value<dtype>, max_value<dtype>) result: dtype = cast(r_clip)\n\nSupported quantized types: `ui1/bool`, `ui2`, `si2`, `ui4`, `si4`, `ui8`, `si8`\n\n**Per-axis quantization:** If `axis` is provided, `scale` and `zero_point` must be 0-D or 1-D tensors that will be broadcast along the specified axis.\n\nNote: Unlike DequantizeOp, QuantizeOp does NOT support N-D blockwise quantization.\n\n**Per-tensor quantization:** If `axis` is not defined and `scale` is a scalar, the operation is per-tensor.\n\n**Specialized formats:** - MX scaling: `f4e2m1`, `f6e3m2`, `f6e2m3`, `f8e4m3`, `f8e5m2` with `f8e8m0` scale and blocksize 32 - NVFP4: `f4e2m1` with `f8e4m3` scale and blocksize 16"
